MODEL     : qwen/qwen3.8-2.4t-a95b
METRIC    : area_delay_product
RESULT    : PASS  |  best cost: 3.733e+06 area_delay_product  (eval 15)

[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Full 8x8 2-D IDCT of a baseline JPEG decoder (ultraembedded/core_jpeg), module
name `jpeg_idct_2d`: the row pass (jpeg_idct_x, OUT_SHIFT=11), a double-buffered
corner-turn (jpeg_idct_transpose with four 32x32 dual-port RAMs), and the column
pass (jpeg_idct_y, OUT_SHIFT=15), composed exactly as in the original decoder
(downstream ready tied high; the row pass accepts no backpressure). Each 1-D
pass is the factorized (AAN-style) algorithm with cosine constants scaled by
4096 (C1..C7 = 4017, 3784, 3406, 2896, 2276, 1567, 799), time-sharing 5
multipliers over an 8-beat schedule. ALL arithmetic is evaluated in 32-bit
signed context with wrap-around; outputs of each pass are arithmetic-shifted
right (11 then 15); the 1/sqrt(2) odd-part scaling is `(x * 181) / 256` where
`*` wraps at 32 bits and `/` is SIGNED division truncating toward zero. This
exact fixed-point behavior is what the golden vectors encode.

Interface (clocked, sync active-high reset `rst`; no backpressure):
  inputs : clk, rst, img_start_i (1-cycle pulse resets the output streamers),
           img_end_i (unused), inport_valid_i, inport_idx_i[2:0],
           inport_data0..3_i[15:0]
  outputs: outport_valid_o, outport_data_o[31:0], outport_idx_o[5:0]

Input protocol (the upstream block serializer's FIXED schedule, reproduced
exactly by the testbench): per row of 8 coefficients c0..c7 (16-bit signed),
8 back-to-back valid beats with idx = 0..7; the four data lanes carry the EVEN
set (c0,c2,c4,c6) on beats idx in {0,2,5,7} and the ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7) on
beats idx in {1,3,4,6}. 8 rows back-to-back form a 64-beat block; between
blocks there are >= 2 idle cycles (valid=0, idx=0, data=0). Pipeline registers
advance every clock regardless of valid — designs must be robust to the gap
pattern exactly as the original is.

Output (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on outport_valid_o; the
k-th streamed value of a block carries outport_idx_o = {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the
column pass emits column-major; the idx label gives the ROW-MAJOR pixel
position) and must match the expected output at that position. The stream of a
block overlaps the next block's input beats (the transpose double-buffers one
full block).

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` contains the
ORIGINAL core_jpeg modules (Apache 2.0), unmodified, under a thin composition
top. It passes all golden vectors (they were generated from it — the original
RTL is the correctness oracle). Study it, then optimize while preserving its
exact behavior. Sibling benchmark: `jpeg_idct_2d_spire` (identical tb.sv +
vectors.dat, bit-exact SpireHDL starting point). The 1-D row pass alone is
also available as the `jpeg_idct_x_*` benchmarks.

NOTE on memories: the cost-evaluation synthesis flow targets a standard-cell
library with no RAM macros, so every behavioral memory in this design (the
transpose buffers, block_out, etc.) is inferred as a register array plus mux
decode REGARDLESS of coding style. Optimizing that decode/mux logic is fair
game; instantiating a hard RAM macro is not possible in this flow.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

wire signed [31:0] block_in_0_1 = {{16{inport_data0_i[15]}}, inport_data0_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = {{16{inport_data1_i[15]}}, inport_data1_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = {{16{inport_data2_i[15]}}, inport_data2_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0_q <= 32'b0;
    mul1_q <= 32'b0;
    mul2_q <= 32'b0;
    mul3_q <= 32'b0;
    mul4_q <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;
    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;
    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;
    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;
    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0;
reg signed [31:0] mul1;
reg signed [31:0] mul2;
reg signed [31:0] mul3;
reg signed [31:0] mul4;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0 <= 32'b0;
    mul1 <= 32'b0;
    mul2 <= 32'b0;
    mul3 <= 32'b0;
    mul4 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0 <= mul0_q;
    mul1 <= mul1_q;
    mul2 <= mul2_q;
    mul3 <= mul3_q;
    mul4 <= mul4_q;
end

reg        out_stg0_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;
end

reg        out_stg1_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;
end

reg        out_stg2_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t0;
reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6;
reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];

assign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data_i
    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i
    ,input           outport_ready_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          inport_accept_o
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data1_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data2_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data3_o
    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o
);



reg         block_wr_q;
reg         block_rd_q;
reg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;
reg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;

wire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};

wire [31:0] outport_data0_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data1_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data2_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data3_w;

wire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);
wire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);
wire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);
wire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram0
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr0_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data0_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram1
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr1_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data1_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram2
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr2_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data2_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram3
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr3_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data3_w)
);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Input Buffer
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg [1:0] block_ready_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else if (img_start_i)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else
begin
    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;
        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;
    end

    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;
        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;
    end
end

assign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// FSM
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
localparam STATE_W           = 2;
localparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;
localparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;
localparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;

reg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;
reg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;

always @ *
begin
    next_state_r = state_q;

    case (state_q)
    STATE_IDLE:
    begin
        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)
            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;
    end
    STATE_SETUP:
    begin
        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;
    end
    STATE_ACTIVE:
    begin
        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)
            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
    end
    default: ;
    endcase

    if (img_start_i)
        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
end

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;
else
    state_q <= next_state_r;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_IDLE)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_SETUP)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'd8;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
begin
    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])
    3'd0: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd1: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd2: ;
    3'd3: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd4: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd5: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd6: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd1;
    3'd7: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    endcase
end

assign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);
assign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];
assign outport_data0_o = outport_data0_w;
assign outport_data1_o = outport_data1_w;
assign outport_data2_o = outport_data2_w;
assign outport_data3_o = outport_data3_w;


endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk0_i
    ,input           rst0_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data0_i
    ,input           wr0_i
    ,input           clk1_i
    ,input           rst1_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data1_i
    ,input           wr1_i

    // Outputs
    ,output [ 31:0]  data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  data1_o
);



//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Dual Port RAM
// Mode: Read First
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
/* verilator lint_off MULTIDRIVEN */
reg [31:0]   ram [31:0] /*verilator public*/;
/* verilator lint_on MULTIDRIVEN */

reg [31:0] ram_read0_q;
reg [31:0] ram_read1_q;


// Synchronous write
always @ (posedge clk0_i)
begin
    if (wr0_i)
        ram[addr0_i][31:0] <= data0_i[31:0];

    ram_read0_q <= ram[addr0_i];
end

always @ (posedge clk1_i)
begin
    if (wr1_i)
        ram[addr1_i][31:0] <= data1_i[31:0];

    ram_read1_q <= ram[addr1_i];
end


assign data0_o = ram_read0_q;
assign data1_o = ram_read1_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_y
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Params
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
#(
     parameter OUT_SHIFT        = 15
    ,parameter INPUT_WIDTH      = 32
)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Ports
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data2_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);




localparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096

// Self-checking testbench for jpeg_idct_2d — the full 8x8 2-D IDCT
// (x-pass -> transpose -> y-pass) of ultraembedded/core_jpeg.
// Reads vectors.dat: one line per 8x8 block = 64 input coefficients (16-bit
// signed, 4 hex digits, row-major c[row][col]) followed by 64 expected outputs
// (32-bit, 8 hex digits, in ROW-MAJOR pixel order — outputs are matched via
// outport_idx_o, which labels each streamed value with its row-major position).
//
// Drive protocol: identical to the jpeg_idct_x benchmark (the upstream block
// serializer's fixed schedule): per row 8 back-to-back valid beats idx=0..7,
// EVEN coefficient set (c0,c2,c4,c6) at idx in {0,2,5,7}, ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7)
// at idx in {1,3,4,6}; 8 rows back-to-back per 64-beat block; >= 2 idle cycles
// between blocks (valid=0, idx=0, data=0).
//
// Output contract (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on
// outport_valid_o; the k-th value of a block carries
// outport_idx_o == {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the y-pass un-transpose permutation) and
// must equal the expected output at that row-major position. Comparison is
// EXACT (integer outputs).
module tb;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic        clk;
  logic        rst;
  logic        img_start;
  logic        inport_valid;
  logic [15:0] inport_data0, inport_data1, inport_data2, inport_data3;
  logic [2:0]  inport_idx;
  logic        outport_valid;
  logic [31:0] outport_data;
  logic [5:0]  outport_idx;

  jpeg_idct_2d dut (
    .clk(clk), .rst(rst),
    .img_start_i(img_start), .img_end_i(1'b0),
    .inport_valid_i(inport_valid),
    .inport_data0_i(inport_data0), .inport_data1_i(inport_data1),
    .inport_data2_i(inport_data2), .inport_data3_i(inport_data3),
    .inport_idx_i(inport_idx),
    .outport_valid_o(outport_valid),
    .outport_data_o(outport_data),
    .outport_idx_o(outport_idx)
  );

  initial clk = 1'b0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  // ---- load vectors.dat: flat token stream, 128 tokens per block ----------
  localparam MAX_BLOCKS = 1024;
  logic [15:0] coeffs [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  logic [31:0] expy   [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  int num_blocks;

  task automatic load_vectors;
    int fd, rc, n;
    logic [31:0] tok;
    logic [31:0] flat [0:MAX_BLOCKS*128-1];
    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    n = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      rc = $fscanf(fd, "%h", tok);
      if (rc == 1) begin
        flat[n] = tok;
        n = n + 1;
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);
    num_blocks = n / 128;
    if (num_blocks * 128 != n)
      $display("WARNING: %0d trailing tokens ignored", n - num_blocks * 128);
    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++)
      for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) begin
        coeffs[b*64+i] = flat[b*128+i][15:0];
        expy[b*64+i]   = flat[b*128+64+i];
      end
  endtask

  // ---- output checker -------------------------------------------------------
  int out_cnt;
  bit checking;
  logic [5:0] k;

  always @(posedge clk) begin
    #1;
    if (checking && outport_valid) begin
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      k = out_cnt % 64;
      if (outport_idx !== {k[2:0], k[5:3]}) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d out=%0d idx=%0d expected_idx=%0d",
                 out_cnt / 64, out_cnt % 64, outport_idx, {k[2:0], k[5:3]});
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
      if (outport_data !== expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx]) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d pos=%0d expected_y=%08x actual_y=%08x",
                 out_cnt / 64, outport_idx, expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx],
                 outport_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
`ifdef DUMP_Y
      $display("Y %02x %08x", outport_idx, outport_data);
`endif
      out_cnt = out_cnt + 1;
    end
  end

  // ---- driver ----------------------------------------------------------------
  task automatic drive_beat(input logic valid, input logic [2:0] kk,
                            input logic [15:0] d0, d1, d2, d3);
    @(negedge clk);
    inport_valid = valid;
    inport_idx   = kk;
    inport_data0 = d0;
    inport_data1 = d1;
    inport_data2 = d2;
    inport_data3 = d3;
  endtask

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    out_cnt = 0;
    checking = 0;
    rst = 1;
    img_start = 0;
    inport_valid = 0;
    inport_idx = 0;
    inport_data0 = 0; inport_data1 = 0; inport_data2 = 0; inport_data3 = 0;

    load_vectors();

    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 1;             // one-cycle image-start pulse
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 0;
    checking = 1;

    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++) begin
      for (int r = 0; r < 8; r++) begin
        for (int kb = 0; kb < 8; kb++) begin
          if (kb == 0 || kb == 2 || kb == 5 || kb == 7)
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],

            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],
                                      coeffs[b*64+r*8+4], coeffs[b*64+r*8+6]);
          else
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+1], coeffs[b*64+r*8+3],

      repeat (2 + (b % 3)) drive_beat(1'b0, 3'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0);
    end

[ASSISTANT]
Now I have a good understanding of the design. Let me analyze the key points:

1. **jpeg_idct_x**: Row pass - AAN butterfly, 5 time-shared multipliers over 8 beats, OUT_SHIFT=11
2. **jpeg_idct_transpose**: 4 dual-port RAMs (32×32), double-buffered corner turn with fixed read address sequence
3. **jpeg_idct_y**: Column pass, same butterfly, OUT_SHIFT=15, outputs idx = {ptr[2:0], ptr[5:3]}

Key insights for optimization:
- All multiplies are by **constants** (C1..C7, 181) → CSD shift-add implementations
- Transpose stores x-pass outputs which are bounded by ±415,803 (< 2^19) → **20-bit storage** is bit-exact
- The read address pattern is fixed: per column only 2 addresses (lo/hi) needed → split RAMs
- Pipeline can be compressed (products computed combinationally at beat time)
- `(x*181)/256` trunc-toward-zero = `((x*181 mod 2^32) + (neg?255:0)) >>> 8`

Let me verify my bound analysis on x-pass outputs and write the optimized design:


  -> create_file(filename='design.sv', content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d — optimized 8x8 2-D IDCT (bit-exact with core_jpeg original)\n//  - x pass / y pass: AAN butterfly, constants implemented as CSD shift-add\n//  - compressed pipeline (products formed combinationally at beat time)\n//  - transpose: 20-bit storage (x-pass outputs bounded < 2^19), split lo/hi\n//    arrays with fixed schedule read sequencing\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// ---------- shared arithmetic: constant multiplies (CSD) ----------\n// C1=4017 = 4096-64-16+1\n// C2=3784 = 4096-256-64+8\n// C3=3406 = 4096-512-256+64+16-2\n// C4=2896 = 4096-1024-128-64+16\n// C5=2276 = 2048+256-32+4\n// C6=1567 = 1024+512+32-1\n// C7=799  = 1024-256+32-1\n// 181     = 256-64-8-2-1\n\nmodule idct_x\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [15:0]   inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [15:0]   inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [15:0]   inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [15:0]   inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [ 2:0]   inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data_o\n    ,output [ 5:0]   outport_idx_o\n);\n\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] mc1(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin mc1 = (((x << 12) - (x << 6)) - (x << 4)) + x; end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] mc2(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin mc2 = (((x << 12) - (x << 8)) - (x << 6)) + (x << 3); end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] mc3(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin mc3 = (((((x << 12) - (x << 9)) - (x << 8)) + (x << 6)) + (x << 4)) - (x << 1); end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] mc4(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin mc4 = ((((x << 12) - (x << 10)) - (x << 7)) - (x << 6)) + (x << 4); end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] mc5(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin mc5 = (((x << 11) + (x << 8)) - (x << 5)) + (x << 2); end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] mc6(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin mc6 = (((x << 10) + (x << 9)) + (x << 5)) - x; end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] mc7(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin mc7 = (((x << 10) - (x << 8)) + (x << 5)) - x; end endfunction\n\n// (x * 181) / 256, product wraps at 32 bits, division truncates toward zero\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] d181(input signed [31:0] x);\nreg [31:0] p;\nbegin\n    p = ((((x << 8) - (x << 6)) - (x << 3)) - (x << 1)) - x; // x*181 mod 2^32\n    if (p[31])\n        p = p + 32'd255;\n    d181 = $signed(p) >>> 8;\nend endfunction\n\nwire signed [31:0] X0 = {{16{inport_data0_i[15]}}, inport_data0_i};\nwire signed [31:0] X1 = {{16{inport_data1_i[15]}}, inport_data1_i};\nwire signed [31:0] X2 = {{16{inport_data2_i[15]}}, inport_data2_i};\nwire signed [31:0] X3 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};\n\nreg signed [31:0] i0, s5, s6, s7;\nreg signed [31:0] t0, t1, t2, t3, t4, t5, t6, t7, t65, t56;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (rst)\n    begin\n        i0 <= 32'b0; s5 <= 32'b0; s6 <= 32'b0; s7 <= 32'b0;\n        t0 <= 32'b0; t1 <= 32'b0; t2 <= 32'b0; t3 <= 32'b0;\n        t4 <= 32'b0; t5 <= 32'b0; t6 <= 32'b0; t7 <= 32'b0;\n        t65 <= 32'b0; t56 <= 32'b0;\n    end\n    else\n    begin\n        case (inport_idx_i)\n        3'd0:\n        begin\n            i0 <= X0 + X2;\n            t3 <= mc2(X1) + mc6(X3);\n        end\n        3'd1:\n        begin\n            s7 <= mc1(X0) + mc7(X3);\n            s6 <= mc5(X2) + mc3(X1);\n            t0 <= mc4(i0);\n        end\n        3'd2:\n        begin\n            i0 <= X0 - X2;\n            t0 <= t0 + t3;\n            t3 <= t0 - t3;\n            t7 <= s6 + s7;\n        end\n        3'd3:\n        begin\n            t4 <= (mc7(X0) - mc1(X3)) + (mc3(X2) - mc5(X1));\n        end\n        3'd4:\n        begin\n            t0 <= mc7(X0) - mc1(X3);\n            s5 <= mc3(X2) - mc5(X1);\n        end\n        3'd5:\n        begin\n            t3 <= mc6(X1) - mc2(X3);\n            t4 <= mc4(i0);\n            t5 <= t0 - s5;\n            t6 <= s7 - s6;\n        end\n        3'd6:\n        begin\n            t1 <= t4 + t3;\n            t2 <= t4 - t3;\n            t65 <= t6 - t5;\n            t56 <= t5 + t6;\n        end\n        default:\n        begin\n            s5 <= d181(t65);\n            s6 <= d181(t56);\n        end\n        endcase\n    end\nend\n\n// ---- block_out write strobes (fixed schedule timing) ----\nwire b3 = inport_valid_i && (inport_idx_i == 3'd3);\nwire b6 = inport_valid_i && (inport_idx_i == 3'd6);\nwire b7 = inport_valid_i && (inport_idx_i == 3'd7);\nreg b3_q, b6_q, b6_qq, b7_q;\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (rst)\n    begin\n        b3_q <= 1'b0; b6_q <= 1'b0; b6_qq <= 1'b0; b7_q <= 1'b0;\n    end\n    else\n    begin\n        b3_q <= b3;\n        b6_q <= b6;\n        b6_qq <= b6_q;\n        b7_q <= b7;\n    end\nend\n\n// outputs of this pass are bounded by +/-415803 (< 2^19): 20-bit storage is exact\nreg [19:0] bo0, bo1, bo2, bo3, bo4, bo5, bo6, bo7, bo_tmp;\n\nwire signed [31:0] w0 = (t0 + t7) >>> 11;\nwire signed [31:0] w7 = (t0 - t7) >>> 11;\nwire signed [31:0] w3 = (t3 + t4) >>> 11;\nwire signed [31:0] w4 = (t3 - t4) >>> 11;\nwire signed [31:0] w2 = (t2 + s5) >>> 11;\nwire signed [31:0] w5 = (t2 - s5) >>> 11;\nwire signed [31:0] w1 = (t1 + s6) >>> 11;\nwire signed [31:0] w6 = (t1 - s6) >>> 11;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (rst)\n    begin\n        bo0 <= 20'b0; bo1 <= 20'b0; bo2 <= 20'b0; bo3 <= 20'b0;\n        bo4 <= 20'b0; bo5 <= 20'b0; bo6 <= 20'b0; bo7 <= 20'b0;\n        bo_tmp <= 20'b0;\n    end\n    else\n    begin\n        if (b3_q)\n        begin\n            bo0 <= w0[19:0];\n            bo_tmp <= w7[19:0];\n            bo3 <= w3[19:0];\n            bo4 <= w4[19:0];\n        end\n        if (b6_qq)\n            bo7 <= bo_tmp;\n        if (b7_q)\n        begin\n            bo2 <= w2[19:0];\n            bo5 <= w5[19:0];\n            bo1 <= w1[19:0];\n            bo6 <= w6[19:0];\n        end\n    end\nend\n\n// ---- output streamer: valid = input valid delayed 8 cycles ----\nreg [7:0] vdly;\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (rst || img_start_i)\n        vdly <= 8'b0;\n    else\n        vdly <= {vdly[6:0], inport_valid_i};\nend\n\nreg [5:0] ptr;\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (rst || img_start_i)\n        ptr <= 6'd0;\n    else if (outport_valid_o)\n        ptr <= ptr + 6'd1;\nend\n\nreg [19:0] bo_rd;\nalways @*\nbegin\n    case (ptr[2:0])\n    3'd0: bo_rd = bo0;\n    3'd1: bo_rd = bo1;\n    3'd2: bo_rd = bo2;\n    3'd3: bo_rd = bo3;\n    3'd4: bo_rd = bo4;\n    3'd5: bo_rd = bo5;\n    3'd6: bo_rd = bo6;\n    default: bo_rd = bo7;\n    endcase\nend\n\nassign outport_valid_o = vdly[7];\nassign outport_data_o  = {{12{bo_rd[19]}}, bo_rd};\nassign outport_idx_o   = ptr;\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Transpose: double-buffered corner turn.\n// Each of 4 banks stores rows {2k, 2k+1}; row 2k at address {blk,0,col},\n// row 2k+1 at {blk,1,col}. Values are 20-bit (x-pass output bound).\n// Fixed read schedule per column j: data = lo[j],hi[j],lo[j],hi[j],hi[j],lo[j],x,x\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct_transpose\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data_i\n    ,input  [ 5:0]   inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data0_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data1_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data2_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data3_o\n    ,output [ 2:0]   outport_idx_o\n);\n\nreg         block_wr_q;\nreg         block_rd_q;\nreg [1:0]   block_ready_q;\n\nwire inport_accept_w = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];\n\nlocalparam ST_IDLE   = 2'd0;\nlocalparam ST_SETUP  = 2'd1;\nlocalparam ST_ACTIVE = 2'd2;\n\nreg [1:0] state_q;\nreg [5:0] rcnt_q;\n\nwire active_w  = (state_q == ST_ACTIVE);\nwire rd_last_w = active_w && (rcnt_q == 6'd63);\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (rst || img_start_i)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n        block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n        block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\n    end\n    else\n    begin\n        if (inport_valid_i && (inport_idx_i == 6'd63) && inport_accept_w)\n        begin\n            block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n            block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;\n        end\n        if (rd_last_w)\n        begin\n            block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;\n            block_rd_q                <= ~block_rd_q;\n        end\n    end\nend\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (rst || img_start_i)\n    begin\n        state_q <= ST_IDLE;\n        rcnt_q  <= 6'd0;\n    end\n    else\n    begin\n        case (state_q)\n        ST_IDLE:\n            if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q])\n                state_q <= ST_SETUP;\n        ST_SETUP:\n            state_q <= ST_ACTIVE;\n        ST_ACTIVE:\n        begin\n            rcnt_q <= rcnt_q + 6'd1;\n            if (rcnt_q == 6'd63)\n                state_q <= ST_IDLE;\n        end\n        default: ;\n        endcase\n    end\nend\n\n// ---- read address generation ----\n// raddr presented during cycle k feeds data for cycle k+1 (registered read)\nwire [2:0] col_w   = rcnt_q[5:3];\nwire [2:0] phase_w = rcnt_q[2:0];\n\nreg        rhi_w;\nreg [2:0]  rcol_w;\nalways @*\nbegin\n    rcol_w = col_w;\n    case (phase_w)\n    3'd0: rhi_w = 1'b1;                    // next need: hi[col]\n    3'd1: rhi_w = 1'b0;                    // next need: lo[col]\n    3'd2: rhi_w = 1'b1;                    // next need: hi[col]\n    3'd3: rhi_w = 1'b1;                    // next need: hi[col]\n    3'd4: rhi_w = 1'b0;                    // next need: lo[col]\n    3'd5: rhi_w = 1'b0;                    // next need: x\n    3'd6: rhi_w = 1'b0;                    // {block_rd_q, rhi_w, rcol_w}\n                              : {block_rd_q, 1'b0, 3'd0};\n\n// ---- storage ----\nwire [4:0] waddr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};\nwire       wr_en_w = [19:0] rdq0, rdq1, rdq2, rdq3;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (wr0_w) mem0[waddr_w] <= inport_data_i[19:0];\n    if (wr1_w) mem1[waddr_w] <= inport_data_i[19:0];\n    if (wr2_w) mem2[waddr_w] <= inport_data_i[19:0];\n    if (wr3_w) mem3[waddr_w] <= inport_data_i[19:0];\n\n    rdq0 <= mem0[raddr_w];\n    rdq1 <= mem1[raddr_w];\n    rdq2 <= mem2[raddr_w];\n    rdq3 <= (OUT_SHIFT=15), 32-bit block_out, idx un-transpose is wiring\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule idct_y\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input function automatic signed [31:0] d181(input signed [31:0] x);
reg [31:0] p;

    p = ((((x << 8) - (x << 6)) - (x << 3)) - (x << 1)) - x; // x*181 mod 2^32

        p = p + 32'd255;

end endfunction

[ASSISTANT]
The failures are due to two width bugs I introduced: (1) transpose DW=19 is too small — x-pass outputs are bounded by ±346,256 which needs 20 signed bits (worst case |out0| = (C4·65536 + (C1+C2+C3+C5+C6+C7)·32768)>>11 = 346,256), and (2) the y-pass `IN_BITS=19`/`BO_BITS=17` are wrong for the same reason (y outputs bounded by ~±271,126 > 2^18). [ASSISTANT]
The failures are due to two width bugs I introduced AND a schedule bug. Let me analyze the schedule bug: In the original, beat 3 computes `t4 = s4+s5`, beat 4 sets `t0 <= s4, s5 <= s5val`, and block_out for idx 3 is written using the *pre-beat-4* t0. In my rewrite I moved `t0 <= s4` to beat 3, clobbering final t0 before the block_out write. 

Correct fix: save `t0f <= t0+t3` at beat 2, use dedicated `s4q/s5q` registers at beat 3 (keeping t0/t3/t7 intact), `t4 <= s4q+s5q` at beat 4, and write block_out idx 3 one cycle later (b3_qq). Let me rewrite:

end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] mc6(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin mc6 = ((x << 10) - x) + ((x << 9) + (x << 5)); end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] mc7(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin mc7 = ((x << 10) + (x << 5)) - ((x << 8) + x); end endfunction\n\n// (x * 181) / 256, product wraps at 32 bits, division truncates toward zero\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] d181(input signed [31:0] x);\nreg [31:0] p;\nbegin\n    p = (x << 8) - (((x << 6) + (x << 3)) + ((x << 1) + x)); // x*181 mod 2^32\n    if (p[31])\n        p = p + 32'd255;\n    d181 = $signed(p) >>> 8;\nend endfunction\n\nwire signed [31:0] X0 = {{(32-IN_BITS){inport_data0_i[IN_BITS-1]}}, inport_data0_i[IN_BITS-1:0]};\nwire signed [31:0] X1 = {{(32-IN_BITS){inport_data1_i[IN_BITS-1]}}, inport_data1_i[IN_BITS-1:0]};\nwire signed [31:0] X2 = {{(32-IN_BITS){inport_data2_i[IN_BITS-1]}}, inport_data2_i[IN_BITS-1:0]};\nwire signed [31:0] X3 = {{(32-IN_BITS){inport_data3_i[IN_BITS-1]}}, inport_data3_i[IN_BITS-1:0]};\n\nreg signed [31:0] i0, s4q, s5q, s5, s6, s7;\nreg signed [31:0] t0, t0f, t1, t2, t3, t4, t5, t6, t7, t65, t56;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (rst)\n    begin\n        i0 <= 32'b0; s4q <= 32'b0; s5q <= 32'b0; s5 <= 32'b0; s6 <= 32'b0; s7 <= 32'b0;\n        t0 <= 32'b0; t0f <= 32'b0; t1 <= 32'b0; t2 <= 32'b0; t3 <= 32'b0;\n        t4 <= 32'b0; t5 <= 32'b0; t6 <= 32'b0; t7 <= 32'b0;\n        t65 <= 32'b0; t56 <= 32'b0;\n    end\n    else\n    begin\n        case (inport_idx_i)\n        3'd0:\n        begin\n            i0 <= X0 + X2;\n            t3 <= mc2(X1) + mc6(X3);          // s3\n        end\n        3'd1:\n        begin\n            s7 <= mc1(X0) + mc7(X3);\n            s6 <= mc5(X2) + mc3(X1);\n            t0 <= mc4(i0);                     // s0\n        end\n        3'd2:\n        begin\n            i0  <= X0 - X2;\n            t0f <= t0 + t3;                    // final t0\n            t3  <= t0 - t3;                    // final t3\n            t7  <= s6 + s7;\n        end\n        3'd3:\n        begin\n            s4q <= mc7(X0) - mc1(X3);          // s4 (data repeated at beat 4)\n            s5q <= mc3(X2) - mc5(X1);          // s5\n        end\n        3'd4:\n        begin\n            t4 <= s4q + s5q;                   // s4 + s5\n        end\n        3'd5:\n        begin\n            t3 <= mc6(X1) - mc2(X3);           // s2\n            t4 <= mc4(i0);                     // s1\n            t5 <= s4q - s5q;                   // s4 - s5\n            t6 <= s7 - s6;\n        end\n        3'd6:\n        begin\n            t1 <= t4 + t3;\n            t2 <= t4 - t3;\n            t65 <= t6 - t5;\n            t56 <= t5 + t6;\n        end\n        default:\n        begin\n            s5 <= d181(t65);\n            s6 <= d181(t56);\n        end\n        endcase\n    end\nend\n\n// ---- block_out write strobes (fixed schedule timing) ----\nwire b3 = inport_valid_i && (inport_idx_i == 3'd3);\nwire b6 = inport_valid_i && (inport_idx_i == 3'd6);\nwire b7 = inport_valid_i && (inport_idx_i == 3'd7);\nreg b3_q, b3_qq, b6_q, b6_qq, b7_q;\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (rst)\n    begin\n        b3_q <= 1'b0; b3_qq <= 1'b0; b6_q <= 1'b0; b6_qq <= 1'b0; b7_q <= 1'b0;\n    end\n    else\n    begin\n        b3_q  <= b3;\n        b3_qq <= b3_q;\n        b6_q  <= b6;\n        b6_qq <= b6_q;\n        b7_q  <= b7;\n    end\nend\n\nreg signed [BO_BITS-1:0] bo0, bo1, bo2, bo3, bo4, bo5, bo6, bo7, bo_tmp;\n\nwire signed [31:0] r0 = (t0f + t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\nwire signed [31:0] r7 = (t0f - t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\nwire signed [31:0] r3 = (t3 + t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\nwire signed [31:0] r4 = (t3 - t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\nwire signed [31:0] r2 = (t2 + s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\nwire signed [31:0] r5 = (t2 - s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\nwire signed [31:0] r1 = (t1 + s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\nwire signed [31:0] r6 = (t1 - s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT;\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (rst)\n    begin\n        bo0 <= 0;   // y-pass un-transpose\nelse\n    assign outport_idx_o = ptr;\nendgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Transpose: double-buffered corner turn.\n// 4 banks x 32 x 20 bits. Write addr {blk, idx[3:0]}; each RAM holds rows\n// {2k, 2k+1} at lo/hi bit of the address. +/-346400 (< 2^19)\n\nreg         block_wr_q;\nreg         block_rd_q;\nreg [1:0]   block_ready_q;\n\nwire inport_accept_w = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];\n\nlocalparam ST_IDLE   = 2'd0;\nlocalparam ST_SETUP  = 2'd1;\nlocalparam ST_ACTIVE = 2'd2;\n\nreg [1:0] state_q;\nreg [5:0] rcnt_q;\n\nwire active_w  = (state_q == ST_ACTIVE);\nwire rd_last_w = active_w && (rcnt_q == 6'd63);\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (rst || img_start_i)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n        block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n        block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\n    end\n    else\n    begin\n        if (inport_valid_i && (inport_idx_i == 6'd63) && inport_accept_w)\n        begin\n            block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n            block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;\n        end\n        if (rd_last_w)\n        begin\n            block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;\n            block_rd_q                <= ~block_rd_q;\n        end\n    end\nend\n\nalways @(posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (rst || img_start_i)\n    begin\n        state_q <= ST_IDLE;\n        rcnt_q  <= 6'd0;\n    end\n    else\n    begin\n        case (state_q)\n        ST_IDLE:\n            if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q])\n                state_q <= ST_SETUP;\n        ST_SETUP:\n            state_q <= ST_ACTIVE;\n        ST_ACTIVE:\n        begin\n            rcnt_q <= rcnt_q + 6'd1;\n beats 0..7): lo, hi, lo, hi, hi, lo, hi, lo[next]\n// raddr presented during cycle k is registered -> provides data for cycle k+1\nwire [2:0] col_w   = rcnt_q[5:3];\nwire [2:0] phase_w = rcnt_q[2:0];\n\nreg        rhi_w;\nreg [2:0]  rcol_w;\nalways @*\nbegin\n    rcol_w = col_w;\n    case (phase_w)\n    3'd0: rhi_w = 1'b1;                    // next need: hi[col]\n    3'd1: rhi_w = 1'b0;                    // next need: lo[col]\n    3'd2: rhi_w = 1'b1;                    // next need: hi[col]\n    3'd3: rhi_w = 1'b1;                    // next need: hi[col]\n    3'd4: rhi_w = 1'b0;                    // next need: lo[col]\n    3'd5: rhi_w = 1'b1;                    // next need: hi[col]\n    3'd6:                                  // next need: lo[col+1]\n    begin\n        rhi_w  = 1'b0;\n        rcol_w = col_w + 3'd1;\n    end\n    default:                               // next need: lo[col+1] (first beat of next col)\n    begin\n        rhi_w  = 1'b0;\n        rcol_w waddr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};\nwire       wr_en_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_w;\nwire       wr0_w   = wr_en_w && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);\nwire       wr1_w   = wr_en_w && {{(32-DW){rdq0[DW-1]}}, rdq0};\nassign outport_data1_o = {{(32-DW){rdq1[DW-1]}}, rdq1};\nassign outport_data2_o = {{(32-DW){rdq2[DW-1]}}, rdq2};\nassign outport_data3_o = {{(32-DW){rdq3[DW-1]}}, [ASSISTANT]
Now the design passes at 4.17M. The critical path (1129ps) is the 4-add-level chain `mc3(X1)+mc5(X2)` at beat 1 of the y-pass. Let me do a major restructure: split all deep CSD chains across beats so every register-to-register path has ≤2 adder levels. This requires careful rescheduling using the repeated odd-set beats (1,3,4):


  -> replace_file(filename='design.sv', content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d — optimized 8x8 2-D IDCT (bit-exact with core_jpeg original)\n//  - AAN butterfly with CSD constant multipliers (balanced 2-level trees)\n//  - all beat-time combinational paths limited to <= 2 adder levels by\n//    spreading products/partials across the 8-beat schedule\n//  - transpose: 20-bit storage (x-pass outputs bounded by +/-346400 < 2^19)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// ---------- one 1-D IDCT pass (x: shift 11, y: shift 15) ----------\n// Beat schedule (idx): even sets on {0,2,5,7}, odd sets on {1,3,4,6}.\n//  idx0: i0=c0+c4; s4q=m7-m1; s5=u3-m5-pair(c3)\n//  idx4: t0f=s0+s3; t3f=s0-s3; t7=s6+s7; t4a=s4q+s5; t5=s4q-s5; t6=s7-s6;\n//        t65=t6-t5; t56=t5+t6\n//  idx5: s1=v-(i1<<7)=C4*(c0-c4); m2=C6*c2; m6=C2*c6; w=partial(181*t65);\n//        s3=partial(181*t56)\n//  idx6: t1=t4a+m2-m6; end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] mc6(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin mc6 = ((x << 10) - x) + ((x << 9) + (x << 5)); end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] mc7(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin mc7 = ((x << 10) + (x << 5)) - ((x << 8) + x); end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] partial4(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin partial4 = ((x << 12) - (x << 10)) + ((x << 4) - (x << 6)); end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] partial181(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin partial181 = ((x << 6) + (x << 3)) + ((x << 1) + x); (p + 32'd255) : p;\n    fix181 = $signed(q) >>> 8;\nend endfunction\n\nwire signed [31:0] X0 = {{(32-IN_BITS){inport_data0_i[IN_BITS-1]}}, inport_data0_i[IN_BITS-1:0]};\nwire signed [31:0] X1 = {{(32-IN_BITS){inport_data1_i[IN_BITS-1]}}, inport_data1_i[IN_BITS-1:0]};\nwire signed [31:0] X2 = {{(32-IN_BITS){inport_data2_i[IN_BITS-1]}}, inport_data2_i[IN_BITS-1:0]};\nwire signed [31:0] X3 = {{(32-IN_BITS){inport_data3_i[IN_BITS-1]}}, inport_data3_i[IN_BITS-1:0]};\n\n// register pool (reused across the 8-beat schedule):\n//  i0,i1 | m2,m6 (later n6=C6*c2, n2=C2*c6) | m1,m7 (later p5,p6 = 181*t65/t56)\n//  m5,u3,w (later q5=partial181(t65)) | s0,s3 (later q6),s7 | v,s6,s4q,s5\n//  t0f,t3f,t7,t4a,t5,t6,t65,t56 | s1 | t1,t2\nreg signed [31:0] i0, i1, m2, m6, m1, m7, m5, u3, w;\nreg signed [31:0] s0, s3, s7, v, s6, s4q, s5;\nreg signed [31:0] t0f, t3f, t7, t4a, t5, t6, t65, t56;\nreg signed [31:0] s1, t1, t2;\n\nwire signed [31:0] pair3 = (X1 << 9) + (X1 << 8); Now I must fix two issues: (1) vdly depth — with my compressed schedule bo group B is written at end of beat-7 cycle, outputs must start ≥ 9 cycles after beat 0, and next block's group A write (beat+5+gap) must not corrupt reads — D=8 works (original had D=11); (2) width-0 replication when BO_BITS=32 is illegal. [ASSISTANT]
Excellent — design3.sv passes at **4.06M**, a new best! Now the critical path is the standalone mc3 CSD tree (depth 3). Next I'll pursue a bigger **area** reduction: merge the x-pass block_out + streamer into the transpose RAM (direct writes). The x pass produces 8 outputs per row on just 2 strobe cycles (4 values at idx3+2, 4 at idx7+2), each going to a fixed column address — so we can write directly into the transpose register arrays, eliminating block_out (180 FFs), the streamer counters, and the 8:1 read mux. Let me create design4.sv:

Edited file: design4.sv

[ASSISTANT]
design5 showed adding registers for delay hurts ADP when overdone. My best is design4 (3.79M) where the critical path is the beat-5 `s2=mc6(X1)-mc2(X3)` chain (depth 3 from rdq). I can eliminate ALL depth-3 paths with a clever reschedule that reuses registers:

- Beats 0,2,5,7 carry the SAME even data; beats 1,3,4,6 the same odd data
- At beat 0: precompute q6=mc6(X1), q2=mc2(X3) (for beat-5 s2), and store mc2(X1)/mc6(X3) into m1a/m7b regs (read at beat 1 for t3, then overwritten with C1/C7 products)
- Move t5,t6 to beat 4, t65/t56 to beat 5; split 181* into partial181(t5/t6) at beat 5 (reusing q6/q2) + combine at beat 6 (reusing m1a/m7b), fix at beat 7

Let me create design6.sv with all paths ≤ depth 2:

[ASSISTANT]
The narrowed C3 passes but area rose slightly (3.7368M vs 3.7331M best). The critical path is now `rdq3→m1b` (mc1, 32-bit depth 2). Let me go further: **narrow-split ALL odd-part products** (s3, s2, s4, s5, s6, s7, s0) into ~24-bit pieces exploiting the exact identity C×x = (C×hi)<<10 + C×lo (valid since all intermediates stay < 2^31 for the bounded data), with register recycling. This makes every beat-1 path ≤25 bits. Let me create design7.sv:


  -> create_file(content="//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// jpeg_idct_2d — optimized 8x8 2-D IDCT (bit-exact with core_jpeg original)\n// design7: narrow-sliced constant multiplies.\n// All operands are bounded (x pass: 16-bit; y pass: |X| <= 346400 < 2^19),\n// so every C*x is decomposed EXACTLY as (C*hi)<<10 + C*lo with 11-bit\n// signed hi / 10-bit unsigned lo. The narrow pieces (~24-bit adders) are\n// computed at beat time into recycled registers and combined one cycle\n// later with a single 32-bit add (depth 1). All values stay below 2^31 in\n// magnitude, so no wrap differences vs the original 32-bit arithmetic.\n// Direct-write transpose from x pass (no x block_out/streamer).\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n\nmodule idct_pass7\n#(\n     parameter OUT_SHIFT = 11\n    ,parameter IN_BITS   = 16\n    ,parameter BO_BITS   = 20\n    ,parameter TRANSPOSE_IDX = 0\n)\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [31:0]   inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [ 2:0]   inport_idx_i\n    // direct-write outputs (x pass)\n    ,output          dw_stro_a_o\n    ,output          dw_stro_c_o\n    ,output [ 2:0]   dw_row_o\n    ,output [19:0]   dw_d0_o, dw_d3_o, dw_d4_o, dw_d7_o\n    ,output [19:0]   dw_d1_o, dw_d2_o, dw_d5_o, dw_d6_o\n    ,input           dw_accept_i\n    // streamed outputs (y pass)\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [31:0]   outport_data_o\n    ,output [ 5:0]   outport_idx_o\n);\n\n// ---- narrow CSD multiplies on 11-bit signed operands ----\n// results bounded: C1: 4017*1024 < 2^22, etc. All fit 24-bit signed sums.\nfunction automatic signed [23:0] cn1(input signed [10:0] x);\nreg signed [23:0] xs;\nbegin xs = x; cn1 = ((xs << 12) + xs) - ((xs << 6) + (xs << 4)); end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [23:0] cn2(input signed [10:0] x);\nreg signed [23:0] xs;\nbegin xs = x; cn2 = ((xs << 12) + (xs << 3)) - ((xs << 8) + (xs << 6)); end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [23:0] cn3(input signed [10:0] x);\nreg signed [23:0] xs;\nbegin xs = x; cn3 = (((xs << 12) + (xs << 6)) + ((xs << 4) - (xs << 1))) - ((xs << 9) + (xs << 8)); end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [23:0] cn4(input signed [10:0] x);\nreg signed [23:0] xs;\nbegin xs = x; cn4 = (((xs << 12) - (xs << 10)) + ((xs << 4) - (xs << 6))) - (xs << 7); end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [23:0] cn5(input signed [10:0] x);\nreg signed [23:0] xs;\nbegin xs = x; cn5 = ((xs << 11) + (xs << 2)) + ((xs << 8) - (xs << 5)); end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [23:0] cn6(input signed [10:0] x);\nreg signed [23:0] xs;\nbegin xs = x; cn6 = ((xs << 10) - xs) + ((xs << 9) + (xs << 5)); end endfunction\nfunction automatic signed [23:0] cn7(input signed [10:0] x);\nreg signed [23:0] xs;\nbegin xs = x; cn7 = ((xs << 10) + (xs << 5)) - ((xs << 8) + xs); end endfunction\n\n// full-width C4 (only used for s1 from the i1 register)\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] mc4(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin mc4 = (((x << 12) - (x << 10)) + ((x << 4) - (x << 6))) - (x << 7); end endfunction\n\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] partial181(input signed [31:0] x);\nbegin partial181 = ((x << 6) + (x << 3)) + ((x << 1) + x); end endfunction\n\n// (p = x*181 mod 2^32) -> p/256 signed, truncating toward zero\nfunction automatic signed [31:0] fix181(input [31:0] p);\nreg [31:0] q;\nbegin\n    q = p[31] ? (p + 32'd255) : p;\n    fix181 = $signed(q) >>> 8;\nend endfunction\n\nwire signed [31:0] X0 = {{(32-IN_BITS){inport_data0_i[IN_BITS-1]}}, inport_data0_i[IN_BITS-1:0]};\nwire signed [31:0] X1 = ## Summary

**Best result: design6.sv at 3.733M area_delay_product (PASS, 20672/20672 checks)** vs baseline starting_point.v at 9.50M — a 2.5x improvement.

### What worked best
1. **CSD constant multipliers**: Replaced all `*` with shift-add trees (all constants: C1-C7, 181), eliminating multipliers entirely.
2. **Compressed pipeline**: Form products combinationally at beat time instead of register→register multiply chains.
3. **Direct-write transpose** (design4/6): The x-pass writes its 8 row outputs directly into the transpose register banks (4 values at idx-3+2, 4 at idx-7+2), eliminating the x-pass block_out storage (180 FFs), streamer counters, and 8:1 read mux. This alone gave ~6% area reduction.
4. **Beat-data reuse for path splitting**: Even data repeats on beats {0,2,5,7}, odd on {1,3,4,6}. Precompute products for later beats (e.g. s2 parts at beat 0) and store them in registers that get reused once consumed — zero extra FFs, depth-3→depth-1 on the critical beat-5 path.
5. **20-bit transpose storage** (x-pass outputs bounded by ±346,400 < 2^19) and 17-bit y block_out (±~60k < 2^17).

### Most impactful optimizations
- Multiplier removal: ~9.5M → ~4.9M
- Balanced CSD trees + path splitting at odd beats: → 4.06M
- Direct-write transpose: → 3.79M
- Register-recycled 181* split (partial181 at beat 5, combine at beat 6, fix181 at beat 7): → 3.73M

### What didn't work
- **Over-splitting into dedicated partial registers** (design.sv full-split, design5.sv): delay improved but area ballooned (+200-400 units), net ADP worse. Area dominates ADP here.
- **Fully narrow-sliced multipliers** (design7.sv): 11-bit hi/lo operand split for ALL products added area (4.52M) and the 205ps clk-to-Q-like launch on the critical path wasn't helped.
- **target_delay tuning**: made no difference (delay is constraint-independent at these values).
- Multiple schedule bugs from aggressive rewrites (t0f clobbering, 181* results needed by next row, narrow-split sign errors, missing ping-pong bit in write enables).

### Lessons learned
1. **Keep a passing checkpoint file** before risky rewrites — I lost the 4.11M design once and had to reconstruct it.
2. Incremental edits to a passing design beat full rewrites; most failures came from rewrites.
3. For ADP, register count is the primary cost driver — recycle registers across beats rather than adding pipeline registers.
4. Trace the original design's arithmetic beat-by-beat before rewriting; the valid pipeline timing (vdly depth, b3_q/b3_qq/b7_q strobes) is tightly coupled to the schedule.
5. Files available: `design6.sv` (best), `design7.sv` (narrow-slice, worse), `design5.sv`, `design4.sv`, `design3.sv`, `design2.sv`.
